  RULE Raven’s Talons — Veteran model: Veteran model with Bolt Pistol and Chainsword replaces both with a pair of Raven’s Talons. They count as a pair of Rending Weapons and grant the normal +1 Attack for two close-combat weapons.
  MODS 

selectionEntry id=r46-rg-talons-character name=Upgrade Lightning Claws to Raven’s Talons type=upgrade hidden=false cost=[('pts', '5')] cons=[('r46-rg-talons-character-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Upgrade Lightning Claws to Raven’s Talons: Raven Guard Character already equipped with a pair of Lightning Claws may upgrade them to Raven’s Talons. They count as a pair of Rending Weapons.
  MODS 

selectionEntry id=r46-rg-hand-cannon name=Fulcrum Hand Cannon type=upgrade hidden=false cost=[('pts', '10')] cons=[('r46-rg-hand-cannon-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Fulcrum Hand Cannon: Raven Guard Independent Character or squad Sergeant replaces Bolt Pistol. 18”, S4 AP4, Pistol, Rending, Concussive.
  MODS 

selectionEntry id=r46-rg-infravisor name=Infravisor type=upgrade hidden=false cost=[('pts', '10')] cons=[('r46-rg-infravisor-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Infravisor: Raven Guard Independent Character or squad Sergeant. Gains Night Vision and +1 BS (max BS6). For Blind Initiative tests, bearer and joined unit count as Initiative 1. |  | Night Vision: Units containing at least one model with this effect ignore Night Fighting restrictions.
  MODS 

selectionEntry id=r46-rg-shroud-character name=Shroud Bombs type=upgrade hidden=false cost=[('pts', '10')] cons=[('r46-rg-shroud-character-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Shroud Bombs: Raven Guard Independent Character. Counts as Defensive Grenades. An enemy attempting to charge the equipped unit must first pass a Leadership test or cannot attempt that charge; Vehicles, Daemons and units containing Night Vision are unaffected.
  MODS 

selectionEntry id=r46-rg-shroud-unit name=Shroud Bombs type=upgrade hidden=false cost=[('pts', '20')] cons=[('r46-rg-shroud-unit-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Shroud Bombs: Raven Guard Recon, Veteran or Mor Deythan Squad. Counts as Defensive Grenades. Charging enemy must pass Leadership test or cannot attempt that charge; Vehicles, Daemons and units containing Night Vision are unaffected.
  MODS 

selectionEntry id=r46-rg-cameleoline name=Cameleoline type=upgrade hidden=false cost=[('pts', '10')] cons=[('r46-rg-cameleoline-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Cameleoline: Raven Guard Independent Character wearing Power, Artificer or Recon Armour. Grants Stealth.
  MODS 

selectionEntry id=r46-rg-trans-character name=Teleportation Transponders type=upgrade hidden=false cost=[('pts', '10')] cons=[('r46-rg-trans-character-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Teleportation Transponders: Raven Guard Independent Character in Terminator Armour. Grants Deep Strike even if the mission would not normally permit it.
  MODS 

selectionEntry id=r46-rg-trans-unit name=Teleportation Transponders type=upgrade hidden=false cost=[('pts', '15')] cons=[('r46-rg-trans-unit-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Teleportation Transponders: Raven Guard unit entirely in Terminator Armour. Grants Deep Strike even if the mission would not normally permit it.
  MODS

  RULE Legiones Astartes: All models have And They Shall Know No Fear as presented in ProHammer Classic and gain the named Legiones Astartes rule of their chosen Legion. They are not Fearless.
  RULE Support Squad: This unit may be selected as a Troops choice, but may not fulfil either of the army' two compulsory Troops selections unless a Legion-specific rule or Rite of War removes this restriction.
  RULE Frag Grenades: Frag Grenades use the normal ProHammer Frag Grenade and Assault Grenade rules. Against vehicles, Frag Grenades have Strength 4 + D6 Armour Penetration.

==== CORE destroyer-unit ====
selectionEntry id=destroyer-unit name=Legion Destroyer Squad type=unit hidden=false cost=[('pts', '70')] cons=[] cats=[('destroyer-unit-cat', 'Elites', 'cat-elites', 'true')]
  RULE Standard Wargear: Every model is equipped with Power Armour, two Bolt Pistols, a Chainsword, Frag Grenades and Rad Grenades unless replaced by an option below.
  RULE Legiones Astartes: All models have the Legiones Astartes special rule and And They Shall Know No Fear as presented in ProHammer Classic. They are not Fearless.
  RULE Dual Pistols: A model with this rule may fire both of its Pistol weapons during the Shooting phase. Both pistols must be fired at the same target. If it fires both pistols, it may not fire another weapon during that Shooting phase.
  RULE Destroyer Cadre: A Legion Destroyer Squad may only be joined by an Independent Character with the Legion Moritat Consul upgrade. No other Independent Character may join the squad.
  RULE Rad Grenades: During any player turn in which the unit charges or is itself charged, enemy non-vehicle models engaged with it suffer -1 Toughness until the end of the Assault phase. This affects Instant Death and is not cumulative.
  RULE Dedicated Transport: Without Jump Packs, the squad may select a Rhino, Drop Pod, Dreadclaw Drop Pod or Land Raider where permitted. Land Raider choices will become selectable when their vehicle entries are added.
  INFO Counter-Attack -> r36-shared-counter-attack-478972a rule

==== CORE hq-praetor ====
selectionEntry id=hq-praetor name=Legion Praetor type=unit hidden=false cost=[('pts', '125')] cons=[('hq-praetor-max', 'max', '2', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[('hq-praetor-foc-cat', 'HQ', 'cat-hq', 'true'), ('r41-praetor-iron-halo-cat', 'Iron Halo — army-wide limit', 'r41-iron-halo-army-limit', None)]
  RULE Standard Wargear: Power Armour, Bolt Pistol, Chainsword, Frag Grenades and Iron Halo. The Iron Halo grants a 4+ Invulnerable Save and is already included in the 125-point cost.
  RULE Legiones Astartes: The model has the Legiones Astartes special rule, also has And They Shall Know No Fear as presented in ProHammer Classic, and is not Fearless unless another rule says otherwise. |  | And They Shall Know No Fear: Automatically passes Regroup tests at the start of the turn and may be used normally for the rest of the turn. If caught by a pursuit, the unit automatically regroups and is re-engaged in close combat, and does not suffer extra wounds from being caught in a pursuit. Immune to Fear. | Fear: Enemy units in base contact must pass a Leadership test before resolving their close combat attacks. If the test fails, those units attack with WS1 in the melee engagement. | Fearless: Automatically passes all Morale and Pinning tests. May not Take Cover! or choose to voluntarily fail a break test.
  RULE Frag Grenades: Uses the normal ProHammer Frag Grenade and Assault Grenade rules. Against vehicles, Frag Grenades have Strength 4 + D6 Armour Penetration.
  RULE Space Marine Armoury: The Praetor may select up to 100 points of weapons and wargear from the Space Marine Armoury. Armour, Mobility, Weapon Replacements and Additional Wargear all count toward the same 100-point limit. All normal Armoury restrictions apply.
  RULE Sorcerers of Prospero — Praetor: In a Thousand Sons Detachment, a Legion Praetor is a Psyker (Mastery Level 2). It selects its psychic powers from the single Psychic Discipline associated with its chosen Prosperine Cult: Pavoni — Biomancy; Raptora — Telekinesis; Corvidae — Divination; Athanaeans — Telepathy; Pyrae — Pyromancy, unless another rule explicitly states otherwise. Activating a Force Weapon counts as the use of a psychic power, and the same power may not be used more than once in the same player turn.

==== CORE hq-centurion ====
selectionEntry id=hq-centurion name=Legion Centurion type=unit hidden=false cost=[('pts', '50')] cons=[('hq-centurion-max', 'max', '2', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[('hq-centurion-foc-cat', 'HQ', 'cat-hq', 'true')]
  RULE Standard Wargear: Power Armour, Bolt Pistol, Chainsword and Frag Grenades.
  RULE Legiones Astartes: The model has the Legiones Astartes special rule, also has And They Shall Know No Fear as presented in ProHammer Classic, and is not Fearless unless another rule says otherwise. |  | And They Shall Know No Fear: Automatically passes Regroup tests at the start of the turn and may be used normally for the rest of the turn. If caught by a pursuit, the unit automatically regroups and is re-engaged in close combat, and does not suffer extra wounds from being caught in a pursuit. Immune to Fear. | Fear: Enemy units in base contact must pass a Leadership test before resolving their close combat attacks. If the test fails, those units attack with WS1 in the melee engagement. | Fearless: Automatically passes all Morale and Pinning tests. May not Take Cover! or choose to voluntarily fail a break test.
  RULE Frag Grenades: Uses the normal ProHammer Frag Grenade and Assault Grenade rules. Against vehicles, Frag Grenades have Strength 4 + D6 Armour Penetration.
  RULE Legion Consuls: A Legion Centurion may be upgraded to no more than one Consul type. A Consul retains the Centurion profile, wargear, special rules and Armoury access unless stated otherwise. Wargear granted by a Consul is included in its upgrade cost and does not count toward the Armoury allowance. Consul restrictions override normal Centurion options.
  RULE Space Marine Armoury: The Centurion may select up to 100 points of weapons and wargear from the Space Marine Armoury. Armour, Mobility, Weapon Replacements and Additional Wargear all count toward the same 100-point limit. All normal Armoury restrictions apply. Consul-granted wargear is included in the Consul cost and does not count against this allowance.
  RULE Sorcerers of Prospero — Independent Character: In a Thousand Sons Detachment, a Legion Centurion and its Consul variants are Psykers (Mastery Level 1) unless another rule grants a higher Mastery Level. Its psychic powers are selected from the single Psychic Discipline associated with its chosen Prosperine Cult: Pavoni — Biomancy; Raptora — Telekinesis; Corvidae — Divination; Athanaeans — Telepathy; Pyrae — Pyromancy. A Librarian Consul follows the normal Librarian upgrade rules; an Epistolary is Mastery Level 2.
